- **stash**: Shelve uncommitted changes and restore them later. With no subcommand, `grit stash` saves local changes (same as `grit stash push`), resets the working tree and index to `HEAD`, and records a stash entry compatible with Git's `refs/stash` format. Skip-worktree paths are not stashed or reset on push, and the bit stays set through pop, matching Git. Unreadable worktree directories produce a warning ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/stash.md) · ★598)
- **stash**: Stash the changes in a dirty working directory away. ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/stash.md) · ★101)
- **stash**: `libra stash` 的目标是临时保存脏工作区并支持恢复、查看、删除和基于 stash 建分支。实现需要覆盖 push/pop/list/apply/drop/show/branch/clear，其中 `show` 提供文件级摘要（`--name-only` / `--name-status`）以及 patch 级差异（`-p` / `--patch`，复用 `log::generate_diff` 输出统一 diff），`push` 支持 `-u` / `--include-untracked`（`--no-include-untracked` 经 clap `overrides_with` 撤销，最后出现者生效；默认排除未跟踪故单独为 no-op）、`-a` / `--all`、`-k` / `--keep-index` 与 `-- &lt;pathspec&gt;...`（只 stash ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/stash.md) · ★101)

- **stash**: Save dirty working-tree + index changes onto a stack and restore them later — faithful to `git stash`. Nested namespace: `repo.stash.{push,list,apply,pop,drop}`. The stack is the `refs/stash` reflog (`stash@{0}` is newest); each entry is a `WIP` commit whose parents are the base commit, the index commit, and (whenever `includeUntracked` is set) an untracked-files commit. ([scolladon/tsgit](https://github.com/scolladon/tsgit/blob/HEAD/docs/use/commands/stash.md) · ★4)

- **stats**: Show squad-mcp observability dashboard for past runs in this workspace. Bar charts, score distribution, verdict mix, sparkline trend, per-agent token + wall-clock cost breakdown. Reads .squad/runs.jsonl; never writes. Triggers: "squad stats", "run history", "score distribution", "where did the tokens go", "how much did squad cost", "agent cost breakdown". Example: "/squad:stats --quick" (last 7d) or "/squad:stats --thorough". Flags: --quick (last 7d), --thorough (full history + per-agent panel), --since &lt;ISO&gt;, --last &lt;N&gt;, --no-color.
